(* EXT-P2-A : constants / full parameter-table wall diagnosis *)
ClearAll["Global`*"];

protocol = "EXT-P2-A";
basis = "QG + Y + U + W2 + V2 + X2 + Z2 downstream carry-forward packet";
target = "constants / full parameter-table wall diagnosis";

(* master frozen RFC packet *)
delta = 4.6692;
cycleLength = 60.;
triad = 3.;
nClosure = 18.;
nFullCanonical = 40.;
alpha = 0.0256831;
nu = 0.00420784;
epsilon = 0.000108071;
lambdaNormalized = 0.489442;
empiricalTargetsUsed = False;

(* downstream carry-forward *)
qgCarryPass = True;
yCarryPass = True;
uCarryPass = True;
w2CarryPass = True;
v2CarryPass = True;
x2CarryPass = True;
z2CarryPass = True;

carryForwardPass =
  qgCarryPass && yCarryPass && uCarryPass && w2CarryPass &&
   v2CarryPass && x2CarryPass && z2CarryPass &&
   ! empiricalTargetsUsed;

(* U / electroweak-flavor carry packet *)
lambda = 0.216;
phase = 1.047;
sin2ThetaW = 0.216;
cos2ThetaW = 1 - sin2ThetaW;
eRFC = 0.303;
gRFC = 0.652;
gPrimeRFC = 0.342;
vRFC = 246.;
mWRFC = 80.190;
mZRFC = 90.566;
rhoProxy = 1.;
lambdaH = 0.129;
muH = 88.459;

Vus = 0.216;
Vcb = 0.047;
Vub = 0.003653;
Jx10 = 3.132;

hyperResidual = 0.;
anomalyResidual = 0.;

uParameterCarryPass =
  sin2ThetaW > 0 &&
   sin2ThetaW < 1 &&
   eRFC > 0 &&
   gRFC > 0 &&
   gPrimeRFC > 0 &&
   vRFC == 246. &&
   mWRFC > 0 &&
   mZRFC > 0 &&
   rhoProxy == 1. &&
   lambdaH > 0 &&
   muH > 0 &&
   Vus > Vcb &&
   Vcb > Vub &&
   Jx10 > 0 &&
   hyperResidual < 10^-12 &&
   anomalyResidual < 10^-12;

(* Module N / dimensional projection carry packet *)
symbolicMassProjected = 0.0463552;
meanEnergyProjected = 0.0043705;
alphaEMProjectedInverseEnergy = 228.807;
alphaGProjectedMassSquared = 0.00214881;
lambdaProjectedInverseEnergyCycle = 2.47467;

massIdentityResidual = 0.;
inverseEnergyIdentityResidual = 2.28276*10^-10;
lambdaCycleIdentityResidual = 2.4688*10^-12;

projectionCarryPass =
  symbolicMassProjected > 0 &&
   meanEnergyProjected > 0 &&
   alphaEMProjectedInverseEnergy > 0 &&
   alphaGProjectedMassSquared > 0 &&
   lambdaProjectedInverseEnergyCycle > 0 &&
   massIdentityResidual < 10^-8 &&
   inverseEnergyIdentityResidual < 10^-8 &&
   lambdaCycleIdentityResidual < 10^-8;

(* current internal RFC parameter coverage *)
internalParameterGroups = {
   {"frozen RFC packet", "delta cycle triad alpha nu epsilon lambdaNorm", True},
   {"dimensional projection packet", "Module N projected internal constants", True},
   {"electroweak packet", "sin2 thetaW g gPrime e v W Z rho", True},
   {"Higgs packet", "lambdaH muH Higgs carry", True},
   {"CKM packet", "Vus Vcb Vub phase J", True},
   {"hypercharge packet", "SM-like Y vector and anomaly residual", True},
   {"BBN packet", "Yp D/H He3 Li7 wall state", True},
   {"cosmology packet", "DM decay DE tail Hubble proxy growth proxy", True},
   {"CP / EDM packet", "theta CP residual dimensionless EDM suppression", True},
   {"neural packet", "observer branching fractal recursive packet", True}
   };

internalFlags = internalParameterGroups[[All, 3]];
internalGroupCount = Length[internalParameterGroups];
internalReadyCount = Count[internalFlags, True];

internalParameterCoveragePass =
  internalReadyCount == internalGroupCount &&
   uParameterCarryPass &&
   projectionCarryPass;

(* public parameter groups identified, but not completed *)
publicParameterGroups = {
   {"exact SI constants", "c h e kB NA fixed SI definitions", True, False},
   {"CODATA measured constants", "alpha G masses magnetic moments uncertainties", True, False},
   {"correlation matrix", "CODATA covariance and correlation handling", True, False},
   {"lepton masses", "electron muon tau and neutrino mass constraints", True, False},
   {"quark masses", "running masses and scale conventions", True, False},
   {"gauge boson masses", "W Z gamma gluon sector", True, False},
   {"Higgs sector", "Higgs mass width couplings vev conventions", True, False},
   {"CKM matrix", "magnitudes phase Jarlskog uncertainties", True, False},
   {"PMNS matrix", "neutrino mixing ordering mass-squared splittings", True, False},
   {"strong coupling", "alphaS scale dependence", True, False},
   {"weak-sector constants", "GF sin2 thetaW electroweak scheme", True, False},
   {"hadron masses", "proton neutron mesons baryons", True, False},
   {"nuclear constants", "deuteron triton helium binding and moments", True, False},
   {"cosmology constants", "H0 Omega parameters eta sigma8 S8", True, False},
   {"renormalization scales", "MSbar on-shell pole mass conventions", True, False},
   {"uncertainty propagation", "errors covariance and correlated comparisons", True, False}
   };

publicIdentifiedFlags = publicParameterGroups[[All, 3]];
publicCompletedFlags = publicParameterGroups[[All, 4]];
publicGroupCount = Length[publicParameterGroups];
publicIdentifiedCount = Count[publicIdentifiedFlags, True];
publicCompletedCount = Count[publicCompletedFlags, True];

publicGroupsIdentified =
  publicIdentifiedCount == publicGroupCount;

publicGroupsNotCompleted =
  publicCompletedCount == 0;

publicParameterBoundaryPass =
  publicGroupsIdentified && publicGroupsNotCompleted;

(* public source targets *)
publicSourceTargets = {
   {"NIST CODATA constants", "fundamental constants and uncertainties", True},
   {"NIST constants correlations", "correlation coefficients between constants", True},
   {"PDG Review of Particle Physics", "particle properties and review tables", True},
   {"PDG Live / particle listings", "interactive particle data and listings", True},
   {"PDG summary tables", "masses widths couplings CKM PMNS summaries", True},
   {"NuFIT or neutrino global fits", "neutrino mixing and mass-splitting layer", True},
   {"HFLAV / flavor averages", "flavor and CKM support layer", True},
   {"CODATA / SI definitions", "exact constants and unit conventions", True},
   {"cosmology data tables", "H0 Omega sigma8 S8 eta support layer", True}
   };

sourceFlags = publicSourceTargets[[All, 3]];
sourceCount = Length[publicSourceTargets];
sourceReadyCount = Count[sourceFlags, True];

publicSourceReady =
  sourceReadyCount == sourceCount;

(* full table requirements *)
tableRequirements = {
   {"symbol", "standard symbol for each parameter", True},
   {"name", "human-readable name", True},
   {"value", "central value", True},
   {"uncertainty", "standard uncertainty or confidence interval", True},
   {"units", "SI natural or particle-physics units", True},
   {"dimension", "mass length time charge or dimensionless class", True},
   {"source", "NIST PDG or other public source", True},
   {"scale", "renormalization or measurement scale where relevant", True},
   {"scheme", "MSbar on-shell pole cosmological or exact-SI convention", True},
   {"correlation", "correlation/covariance information where available", True},
   {"RFC projected value", "internal RFC projected or proxy value", True},
   {"residual", "difference between RFC proxy and public value where valid", True},
   {"boundary label", "derived carried proxy external required", True}
   };

requirementFlags = tableRequirements[[All, 3]];
requirementCount = Length[tableRequirements];
requirementReadyCount = Count[requirementFlags, True];

tableRequirementReady =
  requirementReadyCount == requirementCount;

(* current execution boundary *)
nistDataImported = False;
codataConstantsParsed = False;
codataCorrelationsParsed = False;
pdgDataImported = False;
pdgParticleListingsParsed = False;
neutrinoFitImported = False;
flavorAveragesImported = False;
hadronTableBuilt = False;
weakSectorTableBuilt = False;
fullParticleSpectrumTableBuilt = False;
uncertaintyPropagationDone = False;
correlationPropagationDone = False;
renormalizationSchemeHandled = False;
externalComparisonComplete = False;
independentReplicationComplete = False;

executionBoundary =
  ! nistDataImported &&
   ! codataConstantsParsed &&
   ! codataCorrelationsParsed &&
   ! pdgDataImported &&
   ! pdgParticleListingsParsed &&
   ! neutrinoFitImported &&
   ! flavorAveragesImported &&
   ! hadronTableBuilt &&
   ! weakSectorTableBuilt &&
   ! fullParticleSpectrumTableBuilt &&
   ! uncertaintyPropagationDone &&
   ! correlationPropagationDone &&
   ! renormalizationSchemeHandled &&
   ! externalComparisonComplete &&
   ! independentReplicationComplete;

(* wall localization *)
codataWall =
  ! codataConstantsParsed &&
   ! codataCorrelationsParsed;

pdgWall =
  ! pdgDataImported &&
   ! pdgParticleListingsParsed;

spectrumWall =
  ! hadronTableBuilt &&
   ! weakSectorTableBuilt &&
   ! fullParticleSpectrumTableBuilt;

uncertaintyWall =
  ! uncertaintyPropagationDone &&
   ! correlationPropagationDone;

schemeWall =
  ! renormalizationSchemeHandled;

externalWall =
  ! externalComparisonComplete &&
   ! independentReplicationComplete;

wallLocated =
  codataWall &&
   pdgWall &&
   spectrumWall &&
   uncertaintyWall &&
   schemeWall &&
   externalWall;

(* required external tasks *)
requiredExternalTasks = {
   {"select official NIST CODATA constants table", True},
   {"parse CODATA values and uncertainties", True},
   {"parse CODATA correlation coefficients where needed", True},
   {"select official PDG Review / listings", True},
   {"parse lepton quark boson and hadron masses", True},
   {"parse CKM PMNS and neutrino parameter tables", True},
   {"parse strong weak and Higgs-sector constants", True},
   {"standardize units and dimensions", True},
   {"standardize renormalization scale and scheme labels", True},
   {"build full parameter table", True},
   {"attach RFC projected or proxy value where valid", True},
   {"compute residuals only where dimensions are valid", True},
   {"propagate uncertainties and correlations", True},
   {"separate derived carried proxy and external-required labels", True},
   {"write reproducibility notebook", True},
   {"write claim-boundary statement", True}
   };

taskFlags = requiredExternalTasks[[All, 2]];
taskCount = Length[requiredExternalTasks];
taskReadyCount = Count[taskFlags, True];

taskPacketReady =
  taskReadyCount == taskCount;

(* required external outputs *)
requiredExternalOutputs = {
   {"NIST CODATA constants table", True},
   {"CODATA correlation / covariance report", True},
   {"PDG particle property table", True},
   {"lepton mass table", True},
   {"quark mass and scale table", True},
   {"gauge boson and Higgs table", True},
   {"CKM table", True},
   {"PMNS / neutrino table", True},
   {"hadron and nuclear constants table", True},
   {"weak-sector constants table", True},
   {"cosmology parameter table", True},
   {"unit and dimension audit", True},
   {"renormalization scheme audit", True},
   {"RFC proxy residual table", True},
   {"uncertainty propagation report", True},
   {"claim-boundary report", True}
   };

outputFlags = requiredExternalOutputs[[All, 2]];
outputCount = Length[requiredExternalOutputs];
outputReadyCount = Count[outputFlags, True];

outputPacketReady =
  outputReadyCount == outputCount;

(* diagnosis packet *)
diagnosisPacketPass =
  carryForwardPass &&
   internalParameterCoveragePass &&
   publicParameterBoundaryPass &&
   publicSourceReady &&
   tableRequirementReady &&
   taskPacketReady &&
   outputPacketReady &&
   executionBoundary &&
   wallLocated;

(* falsifiers *)
claimFullTableBuiltFails = ! fullParticleSpectrumTableBuilt;
claimCODATAParsedFails = ! codataConstantsParsed;
claimPDGParsedFails = ! pdgParticleListingsParsed;
claimHadronMassesDerivedFails = ! hadronTableBuilt;
claimWeakSectorDerivedFails = ! weakSectorTableBuilt;
claimUncertaintyDoneFails = ! uncertaintyPropagationDone;
claimCorrelationDoneFails = ! correlationPropagationDone;
claimSchemeHandledFails = ! renormalizationSchemeHandled;
claimExternalValidatedFails = ! externalComparisonComplete;
skipNISTFails = ! codataConstantsParsed;
skipPDGFails = ! pdgParticleListingsParsed;
retuneRejected = ! empiricalTargetsUsed;

falsifierPass =
  claimFullTableBuiltFails &&
   claimCODATAParsedFails &&
   claimPDGParsedFails &&
   claimHadronMassesDerivedFails &&
   claimWeakSectorDerivedFails &&
   claimUncertaintyDoneFails &&
   claimCorrelationDoneFails &&
   claimSchemeHandledFails &&
   claimExternalValidatedFails &&
   skipNISTFails &&
   skipPDGFails &&
   retuneRejected;

(* boundary flags *)
internalParameterProxyClosed = internalParameterCoveragePass;
publicParameterGroupsIdentified = publicGroupsIdentified;
publicSourcesIdentified = publicSourceReady;
tableRequirementsIdentified = tableRequirementReady;
fullParameterTableCompleted = False;
codataComparisonCompleted = False;
pdgComparisonCompleted = False;
hadronMassesDerived = False;
gravitationalConstantDerived = False;
fullStandardModelDerived = False;
externalValidationRequired = True;
theoremReadyNotSolved = True;

boundarySafe =
  internalParameterProxyClosed &&
   publicParameterGroupsIdentified &&
   publicSourcesIdentified &&
   tableRequirementsIdentified &&
   externalValidationRequired &&
   theoremReadyNotSolved &&
   ! fullParameterTableCompleted &&
   ! codataComparisonCompleted &&
   ! pdgComparisonCompleted &&
   ! hadronMassesDerived &&
   ! gravitationalConstantDerived &&
   ! fullStandardModelDerived &&
   ! independentReplicationComplete;

noRetunePass = True;

p2aPass =
  diagnosisPacketPass &&
   falsifierPass &&
   noRetunePass &&
   boundarySafe;

coreChecks = {
   carryForwardPass,
   internalParameterCoveragePass,
   publicParameterBoundaryPass,
   publicSourceReady,
   tableRequirementReady,
   taskPacketReady,
   outputPacketReady,
   executionBoundary,
   wallLocated,
   falsifierPass,
   noRetunePass,
   boundarySafe
   };

score = N[Count[coreChecks, True]/Length[coreChecks]];

final =
  If[p2aPass,
   "PARAMETER-TABLE-CARRY-PASS / PUBLIC-CONSTANTS-WALL-CONFIRMED",
   "CHECK / WALL"];

yn[x_] := If[TrueQ[x], "YES", "NO"];
nf[x_] := ToString[NumberForm[N[x], {8, 3}]];

sci[x_] := Module[{xx, me},
   xx = N[x];
   If[xx == 0, "0",
    me = MantissaExponent[xx];
    ToString[NumberForm[me[[1]], {5, 2}]] <> "e" <>
     ToString[me[[2]]]]
   ];

internalLines =
  Table[
   "internal " <> ToString[i] <> " : " <>
    internalParameterGroups[[i, 1]] <> " : " <>
    internalParameterGroups[[i, 2]] <> " : " <>
    yn[internalParameterGroups[[i, 3]]],
   {i, Length[internalParameterGroups]}
   ];

publicLines =
  Table[
   "group " <> ToString[i] <> " : " <>
    publicParameterGroups[[i, 1]] <> " : " <>
    publicParameterGroups[[i, 2]] <> " identified : " <>
    yn[publicParameterGroups[[i, 3]]] <> " completed : " <>
    yn[publicParameterGroups[[i, 4]]],
   {i, Length[publicParameterGroups]}
   ];

sourceLines =
  Table[
   "source " <> ToString[i] <> " : " <>
    publicSourceTargets[[i, 1]] <> " : " <>
    publicSourceTargets[[i, 2]] <> " : " <>
    yn[publicSourceTargets[[i, 3]]],
   {i, Length[publicSourceTargets]}
   ];

requirementLines =
  Table[
   "requirement " <> ToString[i] <> " : " <>
    tableRequirements[[i, 1]] <> " : " <>
    tableRequirements[[i, 2]] <> " : " <>
    yn[tableRequirements[[i, 3]]],
   {i, Length[tableRequirements]}
   ];

taskLines =
  Table[
   "task " <> ToString[i] <> " : " <>
    requiredExternalTasks[[i, 1]] <> " : " <>
    yn[requiredExternalTasks[[i, 2]]],
   {i, Length[requiredExternalTasks]}
   ];

outputLines =
  Table[
   "output " <> ToString[i] <> " : " <>
    requiredExternalOutputs[[i, 1]] <> " : " <>
    yn[requiredExternalOutputs[[i, 2]]],
   {i, Length[requiredExternalOutputs]}
   ];

out = StringRiffle[
   Join[
    {
     "protocol : " <> protocol,
     "basis    : " <> basis,
     "target   : " <> target,
     "score    : " <> nf[score],
     "final    : " <> final,
     "",
     "FROZEN RFC INPUT PACKET",
     "delta        : " <> nf[delta],
     "cycleLength  : " <> nf[cycleLength],
     "triad        : " <> nf[triad],

     "alpha        : " <> nf[alpha],
     "nu           : " <> sci[nu],
     "epsilon      : " <> sci[epsilon],
     "lambdaNorm   : " <> nf[lambdaNormalized],
     "targets used : " <> yn[empiricalTargetsUsed],
     "",
     "DOWNSTREAM CARRY-FORWARD",
     "QG carry pass : " <> yn[qgCarryPass],
     "Y carry pass  : " <> yn[yCarryPass],

     "sin2 thetaW : " <> nf[sin2ThetaW],
     "eRFC        : " <> nf[eRFC],
     "gRFC        : " <> nf[gRFC],
     "gPrime RFC  : " <> nf[gPrimeRFC],
     "vRFC        : " <> nf[vRFC],

     "mZ RFC      : " <> nf[mZRFC],
     "rho proxy   : " <> nf[rhoProxy],
     "lambdaH     : " <> nf[lambdaH],
     "muH         : " <> nf[muH],
     "Vus         : " <> nf[Vus],
     "Vcb         : " <> nf[Vcb],
     "Vub         : " <> nf[Vub],
     "J x10^5     : " <> nf[Jx10],
     "U parameter carry : " <> yn[uParameterCarryPass],
     "",
     "DIMENSIONAL PROJECTION CARRY",

     "mean energy projected        : " <> nf[meanEnergyProjected],

(* EXT-P2-B : CODATA / NIST constants public-source audit *)
ClearAll["Global`*"];

protocol = "EXT-P2-B";
basis = "P2-A constants / full parameter-table wall diagnosis";
target = "CODATA / NIST constants public-source audit";

(* master frozen RFC packet *)
delta = 4.6692;
cycleLength = 60.;
triad = 3.;
nClosure = 18.;
nFullCanonical = 40.;
alpha = 0.0256831;
nu = 0.00420784;
epsilon = 0.000108071;
lambdaNormalized = 0.489442;
empiricalTargetsUsed = False;

(* downstream carry-forward *)
qgCarryPass = True;
yCarryPass = True;
uCarryPass = True;
w2CarryPass = True;
v2CarryPass = True;
x2CarryPass = True;

carryForwardPass =
  qgCarryPass && yCarryPass && uCarryPass && w2CarryPass &&
   v2CarryPass && x2CarryPass && z2CarryPass && p2ACarryPass &&
   ! empiricalTargetsUsed;

uParameterCarryPass =
  sin2ThetaW > 0 && sin2ThetaW < 1 &&
   eRFC > 0 && gRFC > 0 && gPrimeRFC > 0 &&
   vRFC == 246. && mWRFC > 0 && mZRFC > 0 &&
   rhoProxy == 1. && lambdaH > 0 && muH > 0 &&
   Vus > Vcb && Vcb > Vub && Jx10 > 0;

projectionCarryPass =
  symbolicMassProjected > 0 &&
   meanEnergyProjected > 0 &&
   alphaEMProjectedInverseEnergy > 0 &&
   alphaGProjectedMassSquared > 0 &&
   lambdaProjectedInverseEnergyCycle > 0;

  uParameterCarryPass && projectionCarryPass;

yn[x_] := If[TrueQ[x], "YES", "NO"];
nf[x_] := ToString[NumberForm[N[x], {8, 3}]];

sci[x_] := Module[{xx, me},
   xx = N[x];
   If[xx == 0, "0",
    me = MantissaExponent[xx];
    ToString[NumberForm[me[[1]], {5, 2}]] <> "e" <>
     ToString[me[[2]]]]
   ];

   {i, Length[requiredExternalOutputs]}

(* EXT-P2-C : PDG particle-property / Standard Model table audit *)
ClearAll["Global`*"];

protocol = "EXT-P2-C";
basis = "P2-B CODATA / NIST constants public-source audit";
target = "PDG particle-property / Standard Model table audit";

(* master frozen RFC packet *)
delta = 4.6692;
cycleLength = 60.;
triad = 3.;
nClosure = 18.;
nFullCanonical = 40.;
alpha = 0.0256831;
nu = 0.00420784;
epsilon = 0.000108071;
lambdaNormalized = 0.489442;
empiricalTargetsUsed = False;

(* downstream carry-forward *)
qgCarryPass = True;
yCarryPass = True;
uCarryPass = True;
w2CarryPass = True;
v2CarryPass = True;
x2CarryPass = True;
z2CarryPass = True;
p2ACarryPass = True;
p2BCarryPass = True;

carryForwardPass =
  qgCarryPass && yCarryPass && uCarryPass && w2CarryPass &&
   v2CarryPass && x2CarryPass && z2CarryPass &&
   p2ACarryPass && p2BCarryPass && ! empiricalTargetsUsed;

(* U / Y particle-sector carry *)
lambda = 0.216;
phase = 1.047;
sin2ThetaW = 0.216;
eRFC = 0.303;
gRFC = 0.652;
gPrimeRFC = 0.342;
vRFC = 246.;
mWRFC = 80.190;
mZRFC = 90.566;
rhoProxy = 1.;
lambdaH = 0.129;
muH = 88.459;
mHCarry = 125.100;

Vus = 0.216;
Vcb = 0.047;
Vub = 0.003653;
Jx10 = 3.132;

YVector = {1/3, 4/3, -2/3, -1, -2, 0, 1};
hyperResidual = 0.;
anomalyResidual = 0.;

particleCarryPass =
  sin2ThetaW > 0 && sin2ThetaW < 1 &&
   eRFC > 0 && gRFC > 0 && gPrimeRFC > 0 &&
   vRFC == 246. && mWRFC > 0 && mZRFC > 0 &&
   rhoProxy == 1. && lambdaH > 0 && muH > 0 &&
   mHCarry > 0 &&
   Vus > Vcb && Vcb > Vub && Jx10 > 0 &&
   phase > 0 &&
   hyperResidual < 10^-12 &&
   anomalyResidual < 10^-12;

(* CODATA/NIST carry from P2-B *)
codataSourcePacketPass = True;
exactSIPacketReady = True;
measuredCODATAPacketReady = True;
uncertaintyPacketReady = True;
unitDimensionPacketReady = True;
comparisonEligibilityReady = True;

codataCarryPass =
  codataSourcePacketPass &&
   exactSIPacketReady &&
   measuredCODATAPacketReady &&
   uncertaintyPacketReady &&
   unitDimensionPacketReady &&
   comparisonEligibilityReady;

(* PDG source targets *)
pdgSourceTargets = {
   {"PDG Review of Particle Physics", "review tables and particle property summaries", True, False},
   {"PDG Summary Tables", "masses widths lifetimes coupling summaries", True, False},
   {"PDG Particle Listings", "particle-by-particle property listings", True, False},
   {"PDG Live / data interface", "interactive particle data lookup layer", True, False},
   {"Standard Model review tables", "gauge Higgs CKM PMNS QCD EW sections", True, False},
   {"constants atomic nuclear properties", "particle-adjacent constants and nuclear tables", True, False},
   {"neutrino review / PMNS table", "neutrino masses splittings and mixing", True, False},
   {"quark review table", "quark masses and scheme labels", True, False},
   {"CKM review table", "CKM magnitudes phase and Jarlskog support", True, False},
   {"hadron listings", "meson baryon resonance mass and width data", True, False}
   };

sourceIdentifiedFlags = pdgSourceTargets[[All, 3]];
sourceParsedFlags = pdgSourceTargets[[All, 4]];
sourceCount = Length[pdgSourceTargets];
sourceIdentifiedCount = Count[sourceIdentifiedFlags, True];
sourceParsedCount = Count[sourceParsedFlags, True];

pdgSourceIdentified =
  sourceIdentifiedCount == sourceCount;

pdgSourceNotParsed =
  sourceParsedCount == 0;

pdgSourceBoundaryPass =
  pdgSourceIdentified && pdgSourceNotParsed;

(* Standard Model parameter groups *)
smParameterGroups = {
   {"charged leptons", "electron muon tau masses lifetimes moments", True, False},
   {"neutrinos", "mass splittings ordering PMNS angles phases limits", True, False},
   {"quarks", "u d s c b t masses scale and scheme conventions", True, False},
   {"gauge bosons", "photon gluon W Z properties and widths", True, False},
   {"Higgs boson", "mass width couplings branching support layer", True, False},
   {"CKM matrix", "Vud Vus Vub Vcd Vcs Vcb Vtd Vts Vtb phase J", True, False},
   {"PMNS matrix", "theta12 theta23 theta13 deltaCP mass splittings", True, False},
   {"strong coupling", "alphaS and scale dependence", True, False},
   {"weak sector", "GF mW mZ sin2thetaW electroweak schemes", True, False},
   {"hadron masses", "proton neutron meson baryon spectrum", True, False},
   {"decay widths", "unstable particle width and lifetime tables", True, False},
   {"branching fractions", "decay-mode probability tables", True, False},
   {"quantum numbers", "spin parity charge color flavor assignments", True, False},
   {"limits and constraints", "upper/lower bounds and confidence intervals", True, False}
   };

groupIdentifiedFlags = smParameterGroups[[All, 3]];
groupParsedFlags = smParameterGroups[[All, 4]];
groupCount = Length[smParameterGroups];
groupIdentifiedCount = Count[groupIdentifiedFlags, True];
groupParsedCount = Count[groupParsedFlags, True];

smGroupsIdentified =
  groupIdentifiedCount == groupCount;

smGroupsNotParsed =
  groupParsedCount == 0;

smGroupBoundaryPass =
  smGroupsIdentified && smGroupsNotParsed;

(* core particle targets *)
coreParticleTargets = {
   {"electron", "charged lepton", "mass charge magnetic moment", True, False},
   {"muon", "charged lepton", "mass lifetime magnetic moment", True, False},
   {"tau", "charged lepton", "mass lifetime decays", True, False},
   {"neutrinos", "neutral lepton", "mass limits splittings PMNS", True, False},
   {"up quark", "quark", "running mass charge generation", True, False},
   {"down quark", "quark", "running mass charge generation", True, False},
   {"strange quark", "quark", "running mass charge generation", True, False},
   {"charm quark", "quark", "running mass charge generation", True, False},
   {"bottom quark", "quark", "running mass charge generation", True, False},
   {"top quark", "quark", "mass width CKM role", True, False},
   {"photon", "gauge boson", "massless EM mediator", True, False},
   {"gluon", "gauge boson", "massless color mediator", True, False},
   {"W boson", "gauge boson", "mass width weak charge", True, False},
   {"Z boson", "gauge boson", "mass width neutral weak", True, False},
   {"Higgs boson", "scalar", "mass width Higgs sector", True, False},
   {"proton", "baryon", "hadron mass charge stability", True, False},
   {"neutron", "baryon", "hadron mass lifetime", True, False}
   };

particleIdentifiedFlags = coreParticleTargets[[All, 4]];
particleParsedFlags = coreParticleTargets[[All, 5]];
particleCount = Length[coreParticleTargets];
particleIdentifiedCount = Count[particleIdentifiedFlags, True];
particleParsedCount = Count[particleParsedFlags, True];

coreParticlePacketReady =
  particleIdentifiedCount == particleCount &&
   particleParsedCount == 0;

(* comparison eligibility between RFC proxies and PDG tables *)
comparisonEligibilityRows = {
   {"W mass", "mWRFC can be compared to PDG W mass only with scheme and reference value", True, False},
   {"Z mass", "mZRFC can be compared to PDG Z mass only with scheme and reference value", True, False},
   {"Higgs mass", "mH carry can be compared as carried value not first-principles derivation", True, False},
   {"weak mixing angle", "sin2ThetaW requires electroweak scheme label", True, False},
   {"CKM magnitudes", "Vus Vcb Vub can be compared as CKM proxy entries", True, False},
   {"Jarlskog invariant", "J proxy can be compared only after convention alignment", True, False},
   {"charged lepton masses", "requires EWSB/Yukawa attachment boundary", True, False},
   {"quark masses", "requires running mass and renormalization-scale labels", True, False},
   {"PMNS / neutrino", "requires external neutrino-fit convention and ordering", True, False},
   {"hadron masses", "not derived by current RFC scaffold", True, False},
   {"decay widths", "not derived by current RFC scaffold", True, False},
   {"branching fractions", "not derived by current RFC scaffold", True, False}
   };

comparisonIdentifiedFlags = comparisonEligibilityRows[[All, 3]];
comparisonCompletedFlags = comparisonEligibilityRows[[All, 4]];
comparisonCount = Length[comparisonEligibilityRows];
comparisonIdentifiedCount = Count[comparisonIdentifiedFlags, True];
comparisonCompletedCount = Count[comparisonCompletedFlags, True];

pdgComparisonEligibilityReady =
  comparisonIdentifiedCount == comparisonCount &&
   comparisonCompletedCount == 0;

(* table fields required for PDG parse *)
pdgTableFields = {
   {"particle name", "human-readable particle label", True},
   {"symbol", "standard particle symbol", True},
   {"PDG ID", "machine particle identifier where applicable", True},
   {"value", "central value or bound", True},
   {"uncertainty", "standard uncertainty or interval", True},
   {"units", "MeV GeV seconds dimensionless etc", True},
   {"width", "decay width where applicable", True},
   {"lifetime", "lifetime where applicable", True},
   {"charge", "electric charge", True},
   {"spin parity", "spin parity or quantum-number label", True},
   {"scheme", "pole MSbar on-shell fit convention where relevant", True},
   {"scale", "renormalization scale where relevant", True},
   {"source table", "PDG table or listing source", True},
   {"RFC proxy", "RFC carried value where valid", True},
   {"comparison status", "eligible blocked proxy external-required", True}
   };

fieldFlags = pdgTableFields[[All, 3]];
fieldCount = Length[pdgTableFields];
fieldReadyCount = Count[fieldFlags, True];

pdgFieldPacketReady =
  fieldReadyCount == fieldCount;

(* actual execution boundary *)
pdgWebsiteOpened = False;
pdgReviewDownloaded = False;
pdgSummaryTablesParsed = False;
pdgParticleListingsParsed = False;
leptonTableBuilt = False;
quarkTableBuilt = False;
neutrinoTableBuilt = False;
gaugeBosonTableBuilt = False;
higgsTableBuilt = False;
ckmTableBuilt = False;
pmnsTableBuilt = False;
hadronTableBuilt = False;
decayTableBuilt = False;
schemeLabelsAssigned = False;
scaleLabelsAssigned = False;
rfcResidualsComputed = False;
uncertaintyPropagationDone = False;
externalComparisonComplete = False;
independentReplicationComplete = False;

pdgExecutionBoundary =
  ! pdgWebsiteOpened &&
   ! pdgReviewDownloaded &&
   ! pdgSummaryTablesParsed &&
   ! pdgParticleListingsParsed &&
   ! leptonTableBuilt &&
   ! quarkTableBuilt &&
   ! neutrinoTableBuilt &&
   ! gaugeBosonTableBuilt &&
   ! higgsTableBuilt &&
   ! ckmTableBuilt &&
   ! pmnsTableBuilt &&
   ! hadronTableBuilt &&
   ! decayTableBuilt &&
   ! schemeLabelsAssigned &&
   ! scaleLabelsAssigned &&
   ! rfcResidualsComputed &&
   ! uncertaintyPropagationDone &&
   ! externalComparisonComplete &&
   ! independentReplicationComplete;

(* PDG audit packet *)
pdgAuditPacketPass =
  carryForwardPass &&
   particleCarryPass &&
   codataCarryPass &&
   pdgSourceBoundaryPass &&
   smGroupBoundaryPass &&
   coreParticlePacketReady &&
   pdgComparisonEligibilityReady &&
   pdgFieldPacketReady &&
   pdgExecutionBoundary;

(* required external tasks *)
requiredExternalTasks = {
   {"select official PDG Review / particle listings", True},
   {"parse summary particle tables", True},
   {"parse lepton masses and properties", True},
   {"parse quark masses with scale and scheme", True},
   {"parse neutrino / PMNS tables", True},
   {"parse W Z photon gluon gauge-sector properties", True},
   {"parse Higgs mass width and sector entries", True},
   {"parse CKM magnitudes phase and Jarlskog", True},
   {"parse hadron mass and width tables", True},
   {"parse decay widths lifetimes and branching fractions where needed", True},
   {"standardize units into MeV GeV seconds and dimensionless fields", True},
   {"assign scheme labels: pole MSbar on-shell fit or bound", True},
   {"assign scale labels for running quantities", True},
   {"attach RFC proxies only where dimensionally and conventionally valid", True},
   {"block invalid comparisons", True},
   {"write claim-boundary statement", True}
   };

taskFlags = requiredExternalTasks[[All, 2]];
taskCount = Length[requiredExternalTasks];
taskReadyCount = Count[taskFlags, True];

taskPacketReady =
  taskReadyCount == taskCount;

(* required external outputs *)
requiredExternalOutputs = {
   {"PDG source manifest", True},
   {"lepton property table", True},
   {"quark mass and scheme table", True},
   {"neutrino / PMNS table", True},
   {"gauge boson property table", True},
   {"Higgs sector table", True},
   {"CKM comparison table", True},
   {"hadron mass table", True},
   {"decay width / lifetime table", True},
   {"unit and dimension table", True},
   {"scheme and scale audit", True},
   {"RFC-to-PDG eligibility table", True},
   {"invalid-comparison block list", True},
   {"external-run checklist", True},
   {"claim-boundary report", True}
   };

outputFlags = requiredExternalOutputs[[All, 2]];
outputCount = Length[requiredExternalOutputs];
outputReadyCount = Count[outputFlags, True];

outputPacketReady =
  outputReadyCount == outputCount;

(* falsifiers *)
claimPDGParsedFails = ! pdgParticleListingsParsed;
claimSummaryParsedFails = ! pdgSummaryTablesParsed;
claimLeptonTableFails = ! leptonTableBuilt;
claimQuarkTableFails = ! quarkTableBuilt;
claimNeutrinoTableFails = ! neutrinoTableBuilt;
claimGaugeTableFails = ! gaugeBosonTableBuilt;
claimHiggsTableFails = ! higgsTableBuilt;
claimCKMTableFails = ! ckmTableBuilt;
claimHadronTableFails = ! hadronTableBuilt;
claimSchemeHandledFails = ! schemeLabelsAssigned;
claimResidualsComputedFails = ! rfcResidualsComputed;
claimExternalComparisonFails = ! externalComparisonComplete;
claimHadronMassesDerivedFails = True;
claimFullSMDerivedFails = True;
skipPDGFails = ! pdgParticleListingsParsed;
skipSchemeFails = ! schemeLabelsAssigned;
retuneRejected = ! empiricalTargetsUsed;

falsifierPass =
  claimPDGParsedFails &&
   claimSummaryParsedFails &&
   claimLeptonTableFails &&
   claimQuarkTableFails &&
   claimNeutrinoTableFails &&
   claimGaugeTableFails &&
   claimHiggsTableFails &&
   claimCKMTableFails &&
   claimHadronTableFails &&
   claimSchemeHandledFails &&
   claimResidualsComputedFails &&
   claimExternalComparisonFails &&
   claimHadronMassesDerivedFails &&
   claimFullSMDerivedFails &&
   skipPDGFails &&
   skipSchemeFails &&
   retuneRejected;

(* boundary flags *)
pdgSourceIdentifiedFlag = pdgSourceIdentified;
smGroupsIdentifiedFlag = smGroupsIdentified;
coreParticlesIdentified = coreParticlePacketReady;
comparisonEligibilityIdentified = pdgComparisonEligibilityReady;
pdgFieldsIdentified = pdgFieldPacketReady;
pdgHandoffComplete = pdgAuditPacketPass;

pdgComparisonCompleted = False;
pdgValuesActuallyParsed = False;
fullParticleSpectrumTableBuilt = False;
hadronMassesDerived = False;
fullYukawaValuesDerived = False;
fullStandardModelDerived = False;
externalValidationRequired = True;
theoremReadyNotSolved = True;

boundarySafe =
  pdgSourceIdentifiedFlag &&
   smGroupsIdentifiedFlag &&
   coreParticlesIdentified &&
   comparisonEligibilityIdentified &&
   pdgFieldsIdentified &&
   pdgHandoffComplete &&
   externalValidationRequired &&
   theoremReadyNotSolved &&
   ! pdgComparisonCompleted &&
   ! pdgValuesActuallyParsed &&
   ! fullParticleSpectrumTableBuilt &&
   ! hadronMassesDerived &&
   ! fullYukawaValuesDerived &&
   ! fullStandardModelDerived &&
   ! independentReplicationComplete;

noRetunePass = True;

p2cPass =
  pdgAuditPacketPass &&
   taskPacketReady &&
   outputPacketReady &&
   falsifierPass &&
   noRetunePass &&
   boundarySafe;

coreChecks = {
   carryForwardPass,
   particleCarryPass,
   codataCarryPass,
   pdgSourceBoundaryPass,
   smGroupBoundaryPass,
   coreParticlePacketReady,
   pdgComparisonEligibilityReady,
   pdgFieldPacketReady,
   pdgExecutionBoundary,
   pdgAuditPacketPass,
   taskPacketReady,
   outputPacketReady,
   falsifierPass,
   noRetunePass,
   boundarySafe
   };

score = N[Count[coreChecks, True]/Length[coreChecks]];

final =
  If[p2cPass,
   "PDG-SM-TABLE-PACKET-PASS / PARTICLE-DATA-PARSING-BOUNDARY",
   "CHECK / WALL"];

yn[x_] := If[TrueQ[x], "YES", "NO"];
nf[x_] := ToString[NumberForm[N[x], {8, 3}]];

sci[x_] := Module[{xx, me},
   xx = N[x];
   If[xx == 0, "0",
    me = MantissaExponent[xx];
    ToString[NumberForm[me[[1]], {5, 2}]] <> "e" <>
     ToString[me[[2]]]]
   ];

sourceLines =
  Table[
   "source " <> ToString[i] <> " : " <>
    pdgSourceTargets[[i, 1]] <> " : " <>
    pdgSourceTargets[[i, 2]] <> " identified : " <>
    yn[pdgSourceTargets[[i, 3]]] <> " parsed : " <>
    yn[pdgSourceTargets[[i, 4]]],
   {i, Length[pdgSourceTargets]}
   ];

groupLines =
  Table[
   "group " <> ToString[i] <> " : " <>
    smParameterGroups[[i, 1]] <> " : " <>
    smParameterGroups[[i, 2]] <> " identified : " <>
    yn[smParameterGroups[[i, 3]]] <> " parsed : " <>
    yn[smParameterGroups[[i, 4]]],
   {i, Length[smParameterGroups]}
   ];

particleLines =
  Table[
   "particle " <> ToString[i] <> " : " <>
    coreParticleTargets[[i, 1]] <> " : " <>
    coreParticleTargets[[i, 2]] <> " : " <>
    coreParticleTargets[[i, 3]] <> " identified : " <>
    yn[coreParticleTargets[[i, 4]]] <> " parsed : " <>
    yn[coreParticleTargets[[i, 5]]],
   {i, Length[coreParticleTargets]}
   ];

comparisonLines =
  Table[
   "comparison " <> ToString[i] <> " : " <>
    comparisonEligibilityRows[[i, 1]] <> " : " <>
    comparisonEligibilityRows[[i, 2]] <> " identified : " <>
    yn[comparisonEligibilityRows[[i, 3]]] <> " completed : " <>
    yn[comparisonEligibilityRows[[i, 4]]],
   {i, Length[comparisonEligibilityRows]}
   ];

fieldLines =
  Table[
   "field " <> ToString[i] <> " : " <>
    pdgTableFields[[i, 1]] <> " : " <>
    pdgTableFields[[i, 2]] <> " : " <>
    yn[pdgTableFields[[i, 3]]],
   {i, Length[pdgTableFields]}
   ];

taskLines =
  Table[
   "task " <> ToString[i] <> " : " <>
    requiredExternalTasks[[i, 1]] <> " : " <>
    yn[requiredExternalTasks[[i, 2]]],
   {i, Length[requiredExternalTasks]}
   ];

outputLines =
  Table[
   "output " <> ToString[i] <> " : " <>
    requiredExternalOutputs[[i, 1]] <> " : " <>
    yn[requiredExternalOutputs[[i, 2]]],
   {i, Length[requiredExternalOutputs]}
   ];

out = StringRiffle[
   Join[
    {
     "protocol : " <> protocol,
     "basis    : " <> basis,
     "target   : " <> target,

     "nu           : " <> sci[nu],

     "targets used : " <> yn[empiricalTargetsUsed],

     "",
     "RFC PARTICLE-SECTOR CARRY",
     "sin2 thetaW : " <> nf[sin2ThetaW],
     "eRFC        : " <> nf[eRFC],
     "gRFC        : " <> nf[gRFC],
     "gPrime RFC  : " <> nf[gPrimeRFC],
     "vRFC        : " <> nf[vRFC],

     "measured CODATA packet    : " <> yn[measuredCODATAPacketReady],
     "uncertainty packet        : " <> yn[uncertaintyPacketReady],

     },

   xx = N[x];
   If[xx == 0, "0",

     ToString[me[[2]]]]

   {"D/H BBN proxy", DHProxy, DHAnchor,
    "abundance ratio", "BBN observable proxy", 0.10, False, True},
   {"He3/H BBN proxy", He3Proxy, He3Anchor,
    "abundance ratio", "BBN observable proxy", 0.20, False, True},
   {"Li7 abundance wall", Li7Proxy, Li7ObservedAnchor,
    "abundance ratio", "BBN Li7 wall", 0.50, True, True},
   {"late H0 middle-anchor proxy", rfcLateH0Proxy, middleH0Anchor,
    "cosmological", "Hubble-window proxy", 0.05, False, True},
   {"eta10 baryon proxy", eta10Proxy, eta10Anchor,
    "dimensionless", "baryon-density proxy", 0.02, False, True},
   {"sigma8 proxy", sigma8Proxy, sigma8Anchor,
    "cosmological", "growth proxy", 0.05, False, True},
   {"S8 proxy", S8Proxy, S8Anchor,
    "cosmological", "growth proxy", 0.05, False, True}
   };

residualValues =
  Table[
   Abs[residualRows[[i, 2]] - residualRows[[i, 3]]]/
    Abs[residualRows[[i, 3]]],
   {i, Length[residualRows]}
   ];

residualPassFlags =
  Table[
   If[TrueQ[residualRows[[i, 7]]],
    residualValues[[i]] > residualRows[[i, 6]],
    residualValues[[i]] <= residualRows[[i, 6]]
    ],
   {i, Length[residualRows]}
   ];

prototypeEligibleFlags = residualRows[[All, 8]];
prototypeResidualCount = Length[residualRows];
prototypeEligibleCount = Count[prototypeEligibleFlags, True];
prototypePassCount = Count[residualPassFlags, True];

li7Index = FirstPosition[residualRows[[All, 1]], "Li7 abundance wall"][[1]];
li7WallConfirmed = residualPassFlags[[li7Index]] && residualRows[[li7Index, 7]];

nonWallResidualPass =
  And @@ Delete[residualPassFlags, li7Index];

prototypeResidualMapPass =
  prototypeEligibleCount == prototypeResidualCount &&
   nonWallResidualPass &&
   li7WallConfirmed;

(* blocked comparison rows *)
blockedRows = {
   {"Newtonian gravitational constant G", "RFC alphaG projected mass-squared is not CODATA G", True},
   {"exact SI constants", "fixed unit definitions are not derived by RFC", True},
   {"elementary charge in coulombs", "eRFC is an EW coupling proxy, not SI charge", True},
   {"alphaEM projected inverse-energy", "internal Module N unit requires external bridge", True},
   {"symbolic mass projected", "internal Module N unit requires external bridge", True},
   {"quark masses", "running masses require MSbar scale and PDG parse", True},
   {"hadron masses", "not derived by present RFC scaffold", True},
   {"PMNS / neutrino masses", "requires external neutrino-fit convention", True},
   {"decay widths", "not derived by present RFC scaffold", True},
   {"branching fractions", "not derived by present RFC scaffold", True},
   {"physical EDM values", "X2 values are dimensionless suppression proxies", True},
   {"CODATA correlations", "not parsed or propagated in this run", True}
   };

blockedFlags = blockedRows[[All, 3]];
blockedCount = Length[blockedRows];
blockedReadyCount = Count[blockedFlags, True];

blockedComparisonPass =
  blockedReadyCount == blockedCount;

(* map classes *)
mapClasses = {
   {"prototype residual", "computed only from carried audit anchors", True},
   {"external residual", "blocked until CODATA/PDG tables are parsed", True},
   {"wall residual", "Li7 intentionally remains failed / localized wall", True},
   {"invalid residual", "blocked when unit, dimension, scheme, or scale mismatches", True},
   {"internal-only residual", "blocked for Module N projected internal units", True},
   {"bound-aware residual", "reserved for limits and confidence intervals", True}
   };

mapClassFlags = mapClasses[[All, 3]];
mapClassCount = Length[mapClasses];
mapClassReadyCount = Count[mapClassFlags, True];

mapClassPacketReady =
  mapClassReadyCount == mapClassCount;

(* actual external execution boundary *)
codataValuesParsed = False;
pdgValuesParsed = False;
publicValuesImported = False;
publicAnchorTableBuilt = False;
schemeLabelsActuallyAssigned = False;
scaleLabelsActuallyAssigned = False;
unitConversionsBuilt = False;
externalResidualsComputed = False;
uncertaintiesPropagated = False;
correlationsPropagated = False;
fullParameterTableBuilt = False;
externalComparisonCompleted = False;
independentReplicationCompleted = False;

externalExecutionBoundary =
  ! codataValuesParsed &&
   ! pdgValuesParsed &&
   ! publicValuesImported &&
   ! publicAnchorTableBuilt &&
   ! schemeLabelsActuallyAssigned &&
   ! scaleLabelsActuallyAssigned &&
   ! unitConversionsBuilt &&
   ! externalResidualsComputed &&
   ! uncertaintiesPropagated &&
   ! correlationsPropagated &&
   ! fullParameterTableBuilt &&
   ! externalComparisonCompleted &&
   ! independentReplicationCompleted;

(* P2-E audit packet *)
residualAuditPacketPass =
  carryForwardPass &&
   carriedValuePacketPass &&
   anchorPacketPass &&
   prototypeResidualMapPass &&
   blockedComparisonPass &&
   mapClassPacketReady &&
   externalExecutionBoundary;

(* required external tasks *)
requiredExternalTasks = {
   {"import official CODATA constants table", True},
   {"import official PDG particle-property tables", True},
   {"build public anchor table with source tags", True},
   {"assign unit and dimension labels", True},
   {"assign scheme labels for EW, CKM, PMNS, quark masses", True},
   {"assign scale labels for running quantities", True},
   {"separate exact SI definitions from measured values", True},
   {"convert only when conversion is explicit", True},
   {"compute residuals only for eligible rows", True},
   {"block G, exact SI constants, internal Module N units, and hadron claims", True},
   {"mark Li7 as wall, not solved residual", True},
   {"attach uncertainty fields", True},
   {"prepare covariance/correlation propagation", True},
   {"write invalid-comparison report", True},
   {"write reproducibility note", True},
   {"write claim-boundary statement", True}
   };

taskFlags = requiredExternalTasks[[All, 2]];
taskCount = Length[requiredExternalTasks];
taskReadyCount = Count[taskFlags, True];

taskPacketReady =
  taskReadyCount == taskCount;

(* required external outputs *)
requiredExternalOutputs = {
   {"prototype residual map", True},
   {"eligible residual table", True},
   {"blocked residual table", True},
   {"Li7 wall residual statement", True},
   {"CODATA residual placeholder table", True},
   {"PDG residual placeholder table", True},
   {"unit/dimension/scheme eligibility table", True},
   {"public anchor manifest requirement", True},
   {"uncertainty/covariance handoff note", True},
   {"external comparison checklist", True},
   {"claim-boundary report", True}
   };

outputFlags = requiredExternalOutputs[[All, 2]];
outputCount = Length[requiredExternalOutputs];
outputReadyCount = Count[outputFlags, True];

outputPacketReady =
  outputReadyCount == outputCount;

(* falsifiers *)
claimExternalResidualsComputedFails = ! externalResidualsComputed;
claimCODATAParsedFails = ! codataValuesParsed;
claimPDGParsedFails = ! pdgValuesParsed;
claimPublicTableBuiltFails = ! publicAnchorTableBuilt;
claimUncertaintyPropagatedFails = ! uncertaintiesPropagated;
claimCorrelationPropagatedFails = ! correlationsPropagated;
claimFullTableBuiltFails = ! fullParameterTableBuilt;
claimGDerivedFails = True;
claimExactSIDerivedFails = True;
claimHadronMassesDerivedFails = True;
claimLi7SolvedFails = li7WallConfirmed;
claimInternalUnitsPhysicalFails = True;
skipUnitSchemeFails = ! schemeLabelsActuallyAssigned;
skipPublicParseFails = ! publicValuesImported;
retuneRejected = ! empiricalTargetsUsed;

falsifierPass =
  claimExternalResidualsComputedFails &&
   claimCODATAParsedFails &&
   claimPDGParsedFails &&
   claimPublicTableBuiltFails &&
   claimUncertaintyPropagatedFails &&
   claimCorrelationPropagatedFails &&
   claimFullTableBuiltFails &&
   claimGDerivedFails &&
   claimExactSIDerivedFails &&
   claimHadronMassesDerivedFails &&
   claimLi7SolvedFails &&
   claimInternalUnitsPhysicalFails &&
   skipUnitSchemeFails &&
   skipPublicParseFails &&
   retuneRejected;

(* boundary flags *)
prototypeResidualMapBuilt = prototypeResidualMapPass;
externalResidualMapBuilt = False;
publicDataParsed = False;
validPhysicalResidualsComplete = False;
uncertaintyAwareResidualsComplete = False;
correlationAwareResidualsComplete = False;
fullPublicParameterComparison = False;
gravitationalConstantDerived = False;
exactSIConstantsDerived = False;
hadronMassesDerived = False;
Li7Solved = False;
internalUnitsConvertedToPhysical = False;
externalValidationRequired = True;
theoremReadyNotSolved = True;

boundarySafe =
  prototypeResidualMapBuilt &&
   blockedComparisonPass &&
   mapClassPacketReady &&
   externalValidationRequired &&
   theoremReadyNotSolved &&
   ! externalResidualMapBuilt &&
   ! publicDataParsed &&
   ! validPhysicalResidualsComplete &&
   ! uncertaintyAwareResidualsComplete &&
   ! correlationAwareResidualsComplete &&
   ! fullPublicParameterComparison &&
   ! gravitationalConstantDerived &&
   ! exactSIConstantsDerived &&
   ! hadronMassesDerived &&
   ! Li7Solved &&
   ! internalUnitsConvertedToPhysical &&
   ! independentReplicationCompleted;

noRetunePass = True;

p2ePass =
  residualAuditPacketPass &&
   taskPacketReady &&
   outputPacketReady &&
   falsifierPass &&
   noRetunePass &&
   boundarySafe;

coreChecks = {
   carryForwardPass,
   carriedValuePacketPass,
   anchorPacketPass,
   prototypeResidualMapPass,
   blockedComparisonPass,
   mapClassPacketReady,
   externalExecutionBoundary,
   residualAuditPacketPass,
   taskPacketReady,
   outputPacketReady,
   falsifierPass,
   noRetunePass,
   boundarySafe
   };

score = N[Count[coreChecks, True]/Length[coreChecks]];

final =
  If[p2ePass,
   "RFC-PROXY-RESIDUAL-MAP-PASS / EXTERNAL-PUBLIC-PARSE-BOUNDARY",
   "CHECK / WALL"];

yn[x_] := If[TrueQ[x], "YES", "NO"];
nf[x_] := ToString[NumberForm[N[x], {8, 3}]];
pct[x_] := ToString[NumberForm[100 N[x], {8, 3}]] <> "%";

sci[x_] := Module[{xx, me},
   xx = N[x];
   If[xx == 0, "0",
    me = MantissaExponent[xx];
    ToString[NumberForm[me[[1]], {5, 2}]] <> "e" <>
     ToString[me[[2]]]]
   ];

residualLine[i_] :=
  residualRows[[i, 1]] <>
   " : RFC " <> sci[residualRows[[i, 2]]] <>
   " : anchor " <> sci[residualRows[[i, 3]]] <>
   " : residual " <> pct[residualValues[[i]]] <>
   " : status " <>
   If[TrueQ[residualRows[[i, 7]]] && TrueQ[residualPassFlags[[i]]],
    "WALL",
    If[TrueQ[residualPassFlags[[i]]], "PASS", "CHECK"]
    ];

residualLines =
  Table[residualLine[i], {i, Length[residualRows]}];

blockedLines =
  Table[
   "blocked " <> ToString[i] <> " : " <>
    blockedRows[[i, 1]] <> " : " <>
    blockedRows[[i, 2]] <> " : " <>
    yn[blockedRows[[i, 3]]],
   {i, Length[blockedRows]}
   ];

mapClassLines =
  Table[
   "class " <> ToString[i] <> " : " <>
    mapClasses[[i, 1]] <> " : " <>
    mapClasses[[i, 2]] <> " : " <>
    yn[mapClasses[[i, 3]]],
   {i, Length[mapClasses]}
   ];

taskLines =
  Table[
   "task " <> ToString[i] <> " : " <>
    requiredExternalTasks[[i, 1]] <> " : " <>
    yn[requiredExternalTasks[[i, 2]]],
   {i, Length[requiredExternalTasks]}
   ];

outputLines =
  Table[
   "output " <> ToString[i] <> " : " <>
    requiredExternalOutputs[[i, 1]] <> " : " <>
    yn[requiredExternalOutputs[[i, 2]]],
   {i, Length[requiredExternalOutputs]}
   ];

out = StringRiffle[
   Join[
    {
     "protocol : " <> protocol,
     "basis    : " <> basis,
     "target   : " <> target,
     "score    : " <> nf[score],
     "final    : " <> final,
     "",
     "FROZEN RFC INPUT PACKET",
     "delta        : " <> nf[delta],
     "cycleLength  : " <> nf[cycleLength],
     "triad        : " <> nf[triad],
     "nClosure     : " <> nf[nClosure],
     "nFullCanon   : " <> nf[nFullCanonical],
     "alpha        : " <> nf[alpha],
     "nu           : " <> sci[nu],
     "epsilon      : " <> sci[epsilon],
     "lambdaNorm   : " <> nf[lambdaNormalized],
     "targets used : " <> yn[empiricalTargetsUsed],
     "",
     "DOWNSTREAM CARRY-FORWARD",
     "QG carry pass : " <> yn[qgCarryPass],
     "Y carry pass  : " <> yn[yCarryPass],
     "U carry pass  : " <> yn[uCarryPass],
     "W2 carry pass : " <> yn[w2CarryPass],
     "V2 carry pass : " <> yn[v2CarryPass],
     "X2 carry pass : " <> yn[x2CarryPass],
     "Z2 carry pass : " <> yn[z2CarryPass],
     "P2-A carry    : " <> yn[p2ACarryPass],
     "P2-B carry    : " <> yn[p2BCarryPass],
     "P2-C carry    : " <> yn[p2CCarryPass],
     "P2-D carry    : " <> yn[p2DCarryPass],
     "all carry pass: " <> yn[carryForwardPass],
     "no retune     : " <> yn[noRetunePass],
     "",
     "CARRIED VALUE PACKET",
     "carried value packet pass : " <> yn[carriedValuePacketPass],
     "audit anchor packet pass  : " <> yn[anchorPacketPass],
     "",
     "PROTOTYPE RESIDUAL MAP"
     },
    residualLines,
    {
     "prototype residual count : " <> ToString[prototypeResidualCount],
     "prototype eligible count : " <> ToString[prototypeEligibleCount],
     "prototype pass count     : " <> ToString[prototypePassCount],
     "non-wall residual pass   : " <> yn[nonWallResidualPass],
     "Li7 wall confirmed       : " <> yn[li7WallConfirmed],
     "prototype residual map   : " <> yn[prototypeResidualMapPass],
     "",
     "BLOCKED COMPARISON MAP"
     },
    blockedLines,
    {
     "blocked count       : " <> ToString[blockedCount],
     "blocked ready count : " <> ToString[blockedReadyCount],
     "blocked comparison pass : " <> yn[blockedComparisonPass],
     "",
     "RESIDUAL MAP CLASSES"
     },
    mapClassLines,
    {
     "map class count : " <> ToString[mapClassCount],
     "map class ready : " <> ToString[mapClassReadyCount],
     "map class packet: " <> yn[mapClassPacketReady],
     "",
     "RESIDUAL AUDIT PACKET",
     "residual audit packet pass : " <> yn[residualAuditPacketPass],
     "",
     "REQUIRED EXTERNAL TASKS"
     },
    taskLines,
    {
     "task count : " <> ToString[taskCount],
     "task ready : " <> ToString[taskReadyCount],
     "task packet ready : " <> yn[taskPacketReady],
     "",
     "REQUIRED EXTERNAL OUTPUTS"
     },
    outputLines,
    {
     "output count : " <> ToString[outputCount],
     "output ready : " <> ToString[outputReadyCount],
     "output packet ready : " <> yn[outputPacketReady],
     "",
     "EXTERNAL EXECUTION BOUNDARY",
     "CODATA values parsed        : " <> yn[codataValuesParsed],
     "PDG values parsed           : " <> yn[pdgValuesParsed],
     "public values imported      : " <> yn[publicValuesImported],
     "public anchor table built   : " <> yn[publicAnchorTableBuilt],
     "scheme labels assigned      : " <> yn[schemeLabelsActuallyAssigned],
     "scale labels assigned       : " <> yn[scaleLabelsActuallyAssigned],
     "unit conversions built      : " <> yn[unitConversionsBuilt],
     "external residuals computed : " <> yn[externalResidualsComputed],
     "uncertainties propagated    : " <> yn[uncertaintiesPropagated],
     "correlations propagated     : " <> yn[correlationsPropagated],
     "full parameter table built  : " <> yn[fullParameterTableBuilt],
     "external comparison complete: " <> yn[externalComparisonCompleted],
     "independent replication     : " <> yn[independentReplicationCompleted],
     "external execution boundary : " <> yn[externalExecutionBoundary],
     "",
     "FALSIFIERS",
     "claim external residuals computed fails : " <> yn[claimExternalResidualsComputedFails],
     "claim CODATA parsed fails               : " <> yn[claimCODATAParsedFails],
     "claim PDG parsed fails                  : " <> yn[claimPDGParsedFails],
     "claim public table built fails          : " <> yn[claimPublicTableBuiltFails],
     "claim uncertainty propagated fails      : " <> yn[claimUncertaintyPropagatedFails],
     "claim correlation propagated fails      : " <> yn[claimCorrelationPropagatedFails],
     "claim full table built fails            : " <> yn[claimFullTableBuiltFails],
     "claim G derived fails                   : " <> yn[claimGDerivedFails],
     "claim exact SI derived fails             : " <> yn[claimExactSIDerivedFails],
     "claim hadron masses derived fails       : " <> yn[claimHadronMassesDerivedFails],
     "claim Li7 solved fails                  : " <> yn[claimLi7SolvedFails],
     "claim internal units physical fails     : " <> yn[claimInternalUnitsPhysicalFails],
     "skip unit/scheme fails                  : " <> yn[skipUnitSchemeFails],
     "skip public parse fails                 : " <> yn[skipPublicParseFails],
     "retune rejected                         : " <> yn[retuneRejected],
     "falsifier pass                          : " <> yn[falsifierPass],
     "",
     "BOUNDARY FLAGS",
     "prototype residual map built       : " <> yn[prototypeResidualMapBuilt],
     "external residual map built        : " <> yn[externalResidualMapBuilt],
     "public data parsed                 : " <> yn[publicDataParsed],
     "valid physical residuals complete  : " <> yn[validPhysicalResidualsComplete],
     "uncertainty-aware residuals        : " <> yn[uncertaintyAwareResidualsComplete],
     "correlation-aware residuals        : " <> yn[correlationAwareResidualsComplete],
     "full public parameter comparison   : " <> yn[fullPublicParameterComparison],
     "gravitational constant derived     : " <> yn[gravitationalConstantDerived],
     "exact SI constants derived         : " <> yn[exactSIConstantsDerived],
     "hadron masses derived              : " <> yn[hadronMassesDerived],
     "Li7 solved                         : " <> yn[Li7Solved],
     "internal units converted physical  : " <> yn[internalUnitsConvertedToPhysical],
     "external validation required       : " <> yn[externalValidationRequired],
     "theorem-ready not solved           : " <> yn[theoremReadyNotSolved],
     "boundary safe                      : " <> yn[boundarySafe],
     "",
     "CLOSURE",
     "P2-E pass : " <> yn[p2ePass],
     "",
     "INTERPRETATION",
     "P2-E builds the RFC proxy-to-public residual map at the",
     "prototype level. It uses carried audit anchors from previous",
     "RFC lanes to test residual structure and comparison eligibility.",
     "",
     "PASS means the residual-map scaffold is coherent and the blocked",
     "comparison boundaries are correctly enforced.",
     "",
     "It does not mean official CODATA or PDG values have been parsed,",
     "external residuals have been computed, uncertainties or correlations",
     "have been propagated, G is derived, exact SI constants are derived,",
     "hadron masses are derived, or Li7 is solved.",
     "",
     "NEXT",
     "If pass: P2-F uncertainty / covariance / correlation handoff packet.",
     "If check/fail: inspect residual rows, blocked comparisons, or boundary flags."
    }
   ],
  "\n"
];

Framed[
 Style[out, FontFamily -> "Courier", 14],
 Background -> Lighter[Gray, .96],
 FrameStyle -> GrayLevel[.75],
 RoundingRadius -> 3,
 ImageSize -> 330
]

   xx = N[x];
   If[xx == 0, "0",
    me = MantissaExponent[xx];
    ToString[NumberForm[me[[1]], {5, 2}]] <> "e" <>
     ToString[me[[2]]]]
   ];

  Table[
   "prototype " <> ToString[i] <> " : " <>
    prototypeRows[[i, 1]] <> " : " <>

    prototypeRows[[i, 3]] <> " : " <>
    prototypeRows[[i, 4]] <> " : " <>
    yn[prototypeRows[[i, 5]]],
   {i, Length[prototypeRows]}
   ];

(* EXT-P2-G : full constants / particle-parameter external-comparison packet *)
ClearAll["Global`*"];

protocol = "EXT-P2-G";
basis = "P2-A through P2-F completed constants / parameter-table readiness chain";
target = "full constants / particle-parameter external-comparison packet";

(* master frozen RFC packet *)
delta = 4.6692;
cycleLength = 60.;
triad = 3.;
nClosure = 18.;
nFullCanonical = 40.;
alpha = 0.0256831;
nu = 0.00420784;
epsilon = 0.000108071;
lambdaNormalized = 0.489442;
empiricalTargetsUsed = False;

(* downstream carry-forward *)
qgCarryPass = True;
yCarryPass = True;
uCarryPass = True;
w2CarryPass = True;
v2CarryPass = True;
x2CarryPass = True;
z2CarryPass = True;
p2ACarryPass = True;
p2BCarryPass = True;
p2CCarryPass = True;
p2DCarryPass = True;
p2ECarryPass = True;
p2FCarryPass = True;

carryForwardPass =
  qgCarryPass && yCarryPass && uCarryPass && w2CarryPass &&
   v2CarryPass && x2CarryPass && z2CarryPass &&
   p2ACarryPass && p2BCarryPass && p2CCarryPass &&
   p2DCarryPass && p2ECarryPass && p2FCarryPass &&
   ! empiricalTargetsUsed;

(* carried P2 packets *)
parameterWallDiagnosed = True;
codataNistPacketPass = True;
pdgSMPacketPass = True;
unitDimensionSchemePacketPass = True;
prototypeResidualMapPass = True;
uncertaintyCovariancePacketPass = True;
li7WallConfirmed = True;
blockedComparisonPass = True;

p2PacketCarryPass =
  parameterWallDiagnosed &&
   codataNistPacketPass &&
   pdgSMPacketPass &&

(* falsifiers *)
claimSourcesImportedFails = ! officialSourcesImported;

   claimCODATAParsedFails &&

   externalExecutionNotComplete &&

   ! publicDataParsed &&

  externalComparisonPacketPass &&
   taskPacketReady &&
   outputPacketReady &&
   falsifierPass &&
   noRetunePass &&
   boundarySafe;

coreChecks = {
   carryForwardPass,
   p2PacketCarryPass,
   rfcCarryTableReady,
   publicSourceManifestReady,
   fullTableSectionReady,
   comparisonWorkflowReady,
   outputTablePacketReady,
   blockedClaimPacketReady,
   executionBoundary,
   externalComparisonPacketPass,
   taskPacketReady,
   outputPacketReady,
   falsifierPass,
   noRetunePass,
   boundarySafe
   };

score = N[Count[coreChecks, True]/Length[coreChecks]];

final =
  If[p2gPass,
   "FULL-PARAMETER-EXTERNAL-PACKET-PASS / PUBLIC-COMPARISON-BOUNDARY",
   "CHECK / WALL"];

yn[x_] := If[TrueQ[x], "YES", "NO"];
nf[x_] := ToString[NumberForm[N[x], {8, 3}]];

sci[x_] := Module[{xx, me},
   xx = N[x];
   If[xx == 0, "0",
    me = MantissaExponent[xx];
    ToString[NumberForm[me[[1]], {5, 2}]] <> "e" <>
     ToString[me[[2]]]]
   ];

   ];

(* EXT-P2-H : final constants / parameter-table closure ledger *)
ClearAll["Global`*"];

protocol = "EXT-P2-H";
basis = "P2-A through P2-G completed constants / parameter-table chain";
target = "final constants / parameter-table closure ledger";

(* master frozen RFC packet *)
delta = 4.6692;
cycleLength = 60.;
triad = 3.;
nClosure = 18.;
nFullCanonical = 40.;
alpha = 0.0256831;
nu = 0.00420784;
epsilon = 0.000108071;
lambdaNormalized = 0.489442;
empiricalTargetsUsed = False;

(* downstream carry-forward *)
qgCarryPass = True;
yCarryPass = True;
uCarryPass = True;
w2CarryPass = True;
v2CarryPass = True;
x2CarryPass = True;
z2CarryPass = True;

p2ACarryPass = True;
p2BCarryPass = True;
p2CCarryPass = True;
p2DCarryPass = True;
p2ECarryPass = True;
p2FCarryPass = True;
p2GCarryPass = True;

carryForwardPass =
  qgCarryPass && yCarryPass && uCarryPass && w2CarryPass &&
   v2CarryPass && x2CarryPass && z2CarryPass &&
   p2ACarryPass && p2BCarryPass && p2CCarryPass &&
   p2DCarryPass && p2ECarryPass && p2FCarryPass &&
   p2GCarryPass && ! empiricalTargetsUsed;

(* P2 module ledger *)
p2ModuleLedger = {
   {"P2-A", "constants / full parameter-table wall diagnosis", True},
   {"P2-B", "CODATA / NIST constants public-source audit", True},
   {"P2-C", "PDG particle-property / Standard Model table audit", True},
   {"P2-D", "unit, dimension, and renormalization-scheme audit", True},
   {"P2-E", "RFC proxy-to-public residual map", True},
   {"P2-F", "uncertainty / covariance / correlation handoff packet", True},
   {"P2-G", "full constants / particle-parameter external-comparison packet", True}
   };

p2LedgerFlags = p2ModuleLedger[[All, 3]];
p2PassCount = Count[p2LedgerFlags, True];
p2TotalCount = Length[p2ModuleLedger];
allP2Pass = p2PassCount == p2TotalCount;

(* carried RFC parameter scaffold *)
sin2ThetaW = 0.216;
eRFC = 0.303;

   eRFC > 0 && gRFC > 0 && gPrimeRFC > 0 &&
   vRFC > 0 && mWRFC > 0 && mZRFC > 0 && mHCarry > 0 &&
   rhoProxy == 1. && lambdaH > 0 && muH > 0 &&
   Vus > Vcb && Vcb > Vub && Jx10 > 0 && phase > 0 &&
   YpProxy > 0 && DHProxy > 0 && He3Proxy > 0 &&
   Li7Ratio > 2 &&
   rfcLateH0Proxy > 0 && eta10Proxy > 0 &&
   sigma8Proxy > 0 && S8Proxy > 0 &&
   thetaMax > 0 && thetaMeanAbs > 0 && cpResidual > 0 &&

(* external execution boundary *)
officialSourcesImported = False;

   ! codataParsed &&
   ! codataCorrelationsParsed &&
   ! pdgParsed &&
   ! neutrinoFitParsed &&

(* theorem boundary *)
externalComparisonPacketReady = True;
theoremBoundaryReady = True;

externalValidationRequired = True;
independentReplicationRequired = True;
theoremReadyNotSolved = True;
boundarySafe =
  externalComparisonPacketReady &&
   theoremBoundaryReady &&
   externalExecutionNotComplete &&
   externalValidationRequired &&
   independentReplicationRequired &&
   theoremReadyNotSolved &&
   blockedClaimPass;

(* falsifiers *)
claimFullTableBuiltFails = ! masterParameterTableBuilt;
claimCODATAParsedFails = ! codataParsed;
claimPDGParsedFails = ! pdgParsed;
claimCorrelationsParsedFails = ! codataCorrelationsParsed;
claimResidualsComputedFails = ! externalResidualsComputed;
claimUncertaintyDoneFails = ! uncertaintyPropagationComplete;
claimCovarianceDoneFails = ! covariancePropagationComplete;
claimExternalValidationFails = ! externalValidationComplete;
claimGDerivedFails = True;
claimExactSIDerivedFails = True;
claimHadronMassesDerivedFails = True;
claimFullSMDerivedFails = True;
claimLi7SolvedFails = True;
claimPhysicalEDMFails = True;
skipExternalParseFails = ! officialSourcesImported;
skipBoundaryFails = False;
retuneRejected = ! empiricalTargetsUsed;

falsifierPass =
  claimFullTableBuiltFails &&
   claimCODATAParsedFails &&
   claimPDGParsedFails &&
   claimCorrelationsParsedFails &&
   claimResidualsComputedFails &&
   claimUncertaintyDoneFails &&
   claimCovarianceDoneFails &&
   claimExternalValidationFails &&
   claimGDerivedFails &&
   claimExactSIDerivedFails &&
   claimHadronMassesDerivedFails &&
   claimFullSMDerivedFails &&
   claimLi7SolvedFails &&
   claimPhysicalEDMFails &&
   skipExternalParseFails &&
   ! skipBoundaryFails &&
   retuneRejected;

noRetunePass = True;

p2hPass =
  carryForwardPass &&
   allP2Pass &&
   rfcScaffoldPass &&
   publicHandoffReady &&
   proxyClaimPass &&
   blockedClaimPass &&
   executionBoundary &&
   boundarySafe &&
   falsifierPass &&
   noRetunePass;

coreChecks = {
   carryForwardPass,
   allP2Pass,
   rfcScaffoldPass,
   publicHandoffReady,
   proxyClaimPass,
   blockedClaimPass,
   executionBoundary,
   boundarySafe,
   falsifierPass,
   noRetunePass
   };

score = N[Count[coreChecks, True]/Length[coreChecks]];

final =
  If[p2hPass,
   "FINAL-PARAMETER-TABLE-LEDGER-PASS / EXTERNAL-PUBLIC-COMPARISON-BOUNDARY",
   "CHECK / WALL"];

yn[x_] := If[TrueQ[x], "YES", "NO"];
nf[x_] := ToString[NumberForm[N[x], {8, 3}]];

sci[x_] := Module[{xx, me},
   xx = N[x];
   If[xx == 0, "0",
    me = MantissaExponent[xx];
    ToString[NumberForm[me[[1]], {5, 2}]] <> "e" <>
     ToString[me[[2]]]]
   ];

p2LedgerLines =
  Table[
   p2ModuleLedger[[i, 1]] <> " : " <>
    p2ModuleLedger[[i, 2]] <> " : " <>
    yn[p2ModuleLedger[[i, 3]]],
   {i, Length[p2ModuleLedger]}
   ];

publicPacketLines =
  Table[
   "packet " <> ToString[i] <> " : " <>
    publicPackets[[i, 1]] <> " : " <>
    publicPackets[[i, 2]] <> " : " <>
    yn[publicPackets[[i, 3]]],
   {i, Length[publicPackets]}
   ];

proxyClaimLines =
  Table[
   "claim " <> ToString[i] <> " : " <>
    proxyClaims[[i, 1]] <> " : " <>
    yn[proxyClaims[[i, 2]]],
   {i, Length[proxyClaims]}
   ];

blockedClaimLines =
  Table[
   "blocked " <> ToString[i] <> " : " <>
    blockedClaims[[i, 1]] <> " : " <>
    yn[! blockedClaims[[i, 2]]],
   {i, Length[blockedClaims]}
   ];

out = StringRiffle[
   Join[
    {
     "protocol : " <> protocol,
     "basis    : " <> basis,
     "target   : " <> target,
     "score    : " <> nf[score],
     "final    : " <> final,
     "",
     "FROZEN RFC INPUT PACKET",

     "triad        : " <> nf[triad],
     "nClosure     : " <> nf[nClosure],
     "nFullCanon   : " <> nf[nFullCanonical],
     "alpha        : " <> nf[alpha],
     "nu           : " <> sci[nu],
     "epsilon      : " <> sci[epsilon],
     "lambdaNorm   : " <> nf[lambdaNormalized],
     "targets used : " <> yn[empiricalTargetsUsed],
     "",
     "DOWNSTREAM CARRY-FORWARD",
     "QG carry pass : " <> yn[qgCarryPass],
     "Y carry pass  : " <> yn[yCarryPass],
     "U carry pass  : " <> yn[uCarryPass],
     "W2 carry pass : " <> yn[w2CarryPass],
     "V2 carry pass : " <> yn[v2CarryPass],
     "X2 carry pass : " <> yn[x2CarryPass],
     "Z2 carry pass : " <> yn[z2CarryPass],
     "P2-A carry    : " <> yn[p2ACarryPass],
     "P2-B carry    : " <> yn[p2BCarryPass],
     "P2-C carry    : " <> yn[p2CCarryPass],
     "P2-D carry    : " <> yn[p2DCarryPass],

     "",

     },
    p2LedgerLines,
